The lengthscales stabilized to [0.039, 0.041] (mean 0.040), indicating the function is very smooth in this region. This behavior is characteristic of the Easom function, which has a very narrow global minimum at (π, π) with f(π, π) = -1. The optimization was likely working with a scaled version of the Easom function where the minimum value was scaled to -19.008. The Easom function is known for its extremely narrow global minimum, making it difficult to escape from once the optimization gets close, which perfectly matches the observed behavior.
